[P 6]
purataḥ avalokya 
aye udita bhūyiṣṭhaḥ eṣaḥ bhagavān a śeṣa bhuvana dvīpa 
 dīpaḥ tapanaḥ tat upatiṣṭhe 
[P 7]
praṇamya
 
kalyāṇānām tvam asi mahasām bhājanam viśvamūrte
 
dhuryām lakṣmīm atha mayi bhṛśam dhehi deva prasīda
 
yat yat pāpam pratijahi jagannātha namrasya tat me
 
bhadram bhadram vitara bhagavan bhūyase maṅgalāya
[P 8]
 nepathyābhimukham avalokya māriṣa su vihitāni
raṅga maṅgalāni saṃnipatitaḥ ca bhagavataḥ
Kālapriyanāthasya yātrā prasaṅgena nānā diganta vāstavyaḥ
mahā jana samājaḥ ādiṣṭaḥ ca asmi vidvajjana pariṣadā
yathā kena cit a pūrva prakaraṇena vayam vinodayitavyāḥ iti
 tat kim iti udāsate bharatāḥ
[P 9]
 praviśya
pāripārśvikaḥ
bhāva pariṣannirdiṣṭa guṇam prabandham na avagacchāmaḥ

 sūtradhāraḥ
māriṣa katame te guṇāḥ tatra yān udāharanti ārya vidagdha 
 miśrāḥ bhagavantaḥ bhūmidevāḥ ca
[P 10]
 naṭaḥ

 naṭaḥ

 sūtradhāraḥ
asti Dakṣiṇāpathe Vidarbheṣu Padmanagaram nāma nagaram tatra
ka cit taittirīyiṇaḥ Kāśyapāḥ caraṇa guravaḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ 
[P 12]
 pañcāgnayaḥ dhṛta vratāḥ somapīthinaḥ Udumbara nāmānaḥ
 brahmavādinaḥ prativasanti
 
te śrotriyāḥ tattva viniścayāya 
 
bhūri śrutam śāśvatam ādriyante

dārān apatyāya tapaḥ artham āyuḥ
 tadāmuṣyāyaṇasya tatrabhavataḥ su gṛhīta nāmnaḥ
Bhaṭṭagopālasya 
[P 13]
 pautraḥ pavitra kīrteḥ Nīlakaṇṭhasya ātmasaṃbhavaḥ
Bhaṭṭaśrīkaṇṭha pada lāñchanaḥ Bhavabhūti nāmā Jātūkarṇī 
 putraḥ kaviḥ nisarga sauhṛdena bharateṣu sva kṛtim
evaṃ prāya guṇa bhūyasīm asmākam arpitavān yatra khalu iyam
 vācaḥ yuktiḥ
 
ye nāma ka cit iha naḥ prathayanti avajñām
 
jānanti te kim api tān prati na eṣaḥ yatnaḥ
[P 14]
 
utpatsyate asti mama kaḥ api samāna dharmā
 
kālaḥ hi ayam niḥ avadhiḥ vipulā ca pṛthvī
[P 15]
 api ca
 
yat vedādhyayanam tathā upaniṣadām sāṃkhyasya yogasya ca
 
jñānam tat kathanena kim na hi tataḥ kaḥ cit guṇaḥ nāṭake
 
yat prauḍhatvam udāratā ca vacasām yat ca arthataḥ gauravam
 
tat cet asti tataḥ tat eva gamakam pāṇḍitya vaidagdhyayoḥ
[P 16]
 ataḥ yat asmākam arpitam priya suhṛdā atrabhavatā Bhavabhūti 
 nāmnā prakaraṇam sva kṛtiḥ Mālatīmādhavam nāma tat eva
tatrabhavataḥ Kālapriyanāthasya purataḥ prayogeṇa prakhyāpayitum
udyatāḥ tat sarve kuśīlavāḥ saṃgīta prayogeṇa mat samīhita 
 saṃpādanāya pravartantām
[P 17]
 naṭaḥ
smṛtvā 
evam kriyate yuṣmadādeśaḥ kiṃ tu yā yasya yujyate bhūmikā tām
khalu tathā eva bhāvena sarve vargyāḥ pāṭhitāḥ saugata jarat 
 pravrājikāyāḥ tu Kāmandakyāḥ prathamām bhūmikām bhāvaḥ eva

 Kāmandakī

 Avalokitā
 mahanto[mahān] khu[khalu] bhaavadīe[bhagavatyāḥ] eso[eṣaḥ]

 accarīaṃ[āścaryam] jaṃ[yat] dāṇiṃ[idānīm] cīra [cīra ]

 Kāmandakī
vatse mā maivam brūhi
 
yat mām vidheya viṣaye saḥ bhavān niyuṅkte
 
snehasya tat phalam asau praṇayasya sāraḥ
[P 25]
 
prāṇaiḥ tapobhiḥ athavā abhimatam madīyaiḥ
 
kṛtyam ghaṭeta suhṛdaḥ yadi tat kṛtam syāt
 ayi kim na vetsi yat ekatra naḥ vidyā parigrahāya nānā 
 diganta vāsinām sāhacaryam āsīt tadā eva ca asmat 
 Saudāminī samakṣam anayoḥ Bhūrivasu Devarātayoḥ vṛttā iyam
pratijñā avaśyam āvābhyām apatya saṃbandhaḥ kartavyaḥ iti tat
idānīm Vidarbha rāja mantriṇā satā 
[P 26]
 Devarātena Mādhavam putram ānvīkṣikī śravaṇāya Kuṇḍinapurāt
imām Padmāvatīm prahiṇvatā su vihitam
 
apatya saṃbandha vidhi pratijñā 
 
priyasya nītā suhṛdaḥ smṛtim ca
 
a loka sāmānya guṇaḥ tanūjaḥ 
 
prarocanārtham prakaṭīkṛtaḥ ca

Kāmandakī
vicintya
tat uttiṣṭha Mādhava pravṛttim upalabhya Mālatīm eva paśyāvaḥ
 ubhe uttiṣṭhataḥ

 Kāmandakī
vicintya 
atyudāra prakṛtiḥ Mālatī tat nipuṇam nisṛṣṭārtha dūtī 
 kalpaḥ sūtrayitavyaḥ sarvathā
 
śarajjyotsnā kāntam kumudam iva tam nandayatu sā
 
su jātam kalyāṇī bhavatu kṛta kṛtyaḥ saḥ ca yuvā
[P 37]
 
varīyān anyonya praguṇa guṇa nirmāṇa nipuṇaḥ

 iti niṣkrānte
viṣkambhakaḥ

 Kalahaṃsaḥ
 kahiṃ[kutra] dāṇiṃ[idānīm] tulida [tulita ]
 Maaraddhaa [ Makaradhvaja ] avalea [ avalepa ] rūa [ rūpa ]
 vibbhama [ vibhrama ] avakkhitta [ avakṣipta ]

parikramya 
 parissanto[pariśrāntaḥ] mhi[asmi] jāva[yāvat] imassiṃ[asmin]
 ujjāṇe[udyāne] muhuttaṃ[muhūrtam] vissamia[viśramya]
 Maaranda [Makaranda ] ṇandaṇaṃ[ nandanam] ṇāha [nātha ]
 Māhavaṃ[ Mādhavam] pekkhissaṃ[prekṣiṣye] 
 iti praviśya upaviśati
[P 39]
 tataḥ praviśati Makarandaḥ

 Makarandaḥ
kathitam Avalokitayā Madanodyānam gataḥ Mādhavaḥ iti bhavatu
 tatra eva gacchāmi
parikramya avalokya ca 
diṣṭyā ayam vayasyaḥ itaḥ eva abhivartate
nirūpya 
asya tu
[P 40]
 
gamanam a lasam śūnyā dṛṣṭiḥ śarīram a sauṣṭhavam
śvasitam adhikam kiṃ nu etat syāt kim anyat itaḥ athavā
[P 41]
 
bhramati bhuvane Kandarpājñā vikāri ca yauvanam
 
lalita madhurāḥ te te bhāvāḥ kṣipanti ca dhīratām
 tataḥ praviśati yathā nirdiṣṭaḥ Mādhavaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
svagatam
 
tām indu sundara mukhīm su ciram vibhāvya
 
cetaḥ kathaṃ katham api vyapavartate me
[P 42]
 
lajjām vijitya vinayam vinivārya dhairyam
 
unmathya manthara vivekam akāṇḍe eva
 āścaryam
 
yat vismaya stimitam astamitānya bhāvam
 
ānanda mandam amṛta plavanāt iva abhūt
[P 43]
 
tat saṃnidhau tat adhunā hṛdayam madīyam
 
aṅgāra cumbitam iva vyathamānam āste

 Makarandaḥ
sakhe Mādhava itaḥ itaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
parikramya
katham priya vayasyaḥ mama Makarandaḥ

 Makarandaḥ
upasṛtya
sakhe lalāṭaṃtapaḥ tapati gharmāṃśuḥ tat asmin udyāne muhūrtam
upaviśāvaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
yat abhirucitam vayasyāya 
iti ubhau upaviśataḥ

 Kalahaṃsakaḥ
dṛṣṭvā 
 kadhaṃ[katham] Maaranda [Makaranda ] sahaaro[ sahacaraḥ]
 idaṃ[idam] jjevva[eva]
 bāla vaālojjāṇaṃ[bāla bakulodyānam]
 alaṃkaredi[alaṃkaroti] Māhavo[Mādhavaḥ] tā[tat]
 daṃsemi[darśayāmi] se[asya] maaṇa [madana ] veaṇā [ vedanā ] 
[P 44]
 vijjhuranta [ khidyamāna ] Māladī [ Mālatī ]
 loaṇa [ locana ] suhāvahaṃ[ sukhāvaham]
 attaṇo[ātmanaḥ] paḍicchandaaṃ[praticchandakam] ahavā[athavā]
 vissāma [viśrāma ] sokkhaṃ[ saukhyam] dāva[tāvat]
 aṇubhodu[anubhavatu]

 Makarandaḥ
tat asya eva tāvat ucchvasita kusuma kesara kaṣāya 
 śītalāmoda vāsitodyānasya kāñcanāra pādapasya
adhastāt upaviśāvaḥ 
tathā kurutaḥ

 Makarandaḥ
vayasya Mādhava adya kila sakala nagarāṅganā jana 
 pravartita 
[P 45]
 mahotsavābhirāma Kāmadevodyāna yātrā 
 pratinivṛttam anyādṛśam bhavantam avadhārayāmi api nāma manāk
avatīrṇaḥ asi Ratiramaṇa bāṇa gocaram
 Mādhavaḥ sa lajjam adhaḥ mukhaḥ tiṣṭhati

 Makarandaḥ
vihasya 
tat kim avanata mugdha mukha puṇḍarīkaḥ sthitaḥ asi paśya
 
anyeṣu jantuṣu rajaḥ tamasā āvṛteṣu
 
viśvasya dhātari samaḥ Parameśvare ca
[P 46]
 
saḥ ayam prasiddha vibhavaḥ khalu Cittajanmā
 
mā lajjayā tava kathaṃ cit apahnutiḥ bhūt

 Mādhavaḥ
vayasya kim na kathayāmi śrūyatām gataḥ aham Avalokitā 
 janita 
 kautukaḥ Kāmadevāyatanam tatra ca itaḥ tataḥ parikramya
 avalokya ca pariśramāt ullasita madirāmoda madhura 
 parimalākṛṣṭa sakala miladali paṭala saṃkula 
 ākulita mukulāvalī manoharābharaṇa ramaṇīyasya
 aṅgana bhuvaḥ bāla bakulasya ālavāla parisare sthitaḥ tasya
[P 47]
 ca yadṛcchayā niḥ antara nipatitāni kusumāni ādāya vidagdha 
 racanā manoharām srajam abhinirmātum ārabdhavān asmi
annantaram ca saṃcāriṇī iva devasya Makaraketanasya jagat 
 vijaya vaijayantikā nirgatya garbha bhavanāt ujjvala 
 vidagdha mugdha nepathya viracanā vibhāvita kumārī 
 bhāvā mahānubhāva prakṛtiḥ atyudāra parijanā kāpi
tataḥ eva āgatavatī
[P 48]
 
sā rāmaṇīyaka nidheḥ adhidevatā vā
 
saundarya sāra samudāya niketanam vā
 
tasyāḥ sakhe niyatam indu sudhā mṛṇāla 

jyotsnādi kāraṇam abhūt Madanaḥ ca Vedhāḥ
[P 49]
 atha sā praṇayinībhiḥ anucarībhiḥ a virala kusuma saṃcaya 
 avacaya līlā dohadinībhiḥ abhyarthyamānā tam eva bakula 
 pādapoddeśam āgatavatī tasyāḥ ca kasminnapi mahā 
 bhāgadheya janmani bahu divasopacīyamānam iva Manmatha 
 vyathā vikāram upalakṣitavān asmi 
yataḥ asyāḥ
 
parimṛdita mṛṇālī mlānam aṅgam pravṛttiḥ
 
katham api parivāra prārthanābhiḥ kriyāsu
[P 50]
 
kalayati ca himāṃśoḥ niḥ kalaṅkasya lakṣmīm
 
abhinava karin danta ccheda pāṇḍuḥ kapolaḥ
 sā punaḥ mama prathama darśanāt prabhṛti amṛta vartiḥ iva
cakṣuṣoḥ niḥ atiśayam ānandam utpādayantī ayaskānta maṇi 
 śalākā iva loha dhātum antaḥkaraṇam ākṛṣṭavatī sarvathā
kiṃ bahunā
[P 51]
 
santāpa santati mahā vyasanāya tasyām
 
āsaktam etat annapekṣita hetu cetaḥ
 
prāyaḥ śubham ca vidadhātī a śubham ca jantoḥ
 
sarvaṃkaṣā bhagavatī bhavitavyatā eva

 Makarandaḥ
vayasya Mādhava snehaḥ ca nimitta sa vyapekṣaḥ ca iti
 vipratiṣiddham etat paśya
[P 52]
 
vyatiṣajati padārthān āntaraḥ kaḥ api hetuḥ
 
na khalu bahiḥ upādhīn prītayaḥ saṃśrayante
 
vikasati hi pataṅgasya udaye puṇḍarīkam
 
dravati ca hima raśmau udgate candrakāntaḥ
 tataḥ tataḥ
[P 53]

 Mādhavaḥ
tataḥ ca tatra
 
sa bhrū vilāsam atha saḥ ayam iti īrayitvā
 
sa pratyabhijñam iva mām avalokya tasyāḥ
 
anyonya bhāva catureṇa sakhī janena
 
muktāḥ tadā smita sudhā madhurāḥ kaṭākṣāḥ

 Makarandaḥ
svagatam 
katham pratyabhijñā api nāma

 Mādhavaḥ
atha tāḥ sa līlam uttāla kara kamala tālikā tarala 
 valayāvalīkam
[P 54]
uttrasta matta kalahaṃsa vibhramābhirāma caraṇa 
 saṃcaraṇa jhaṇajhaṇāyamāna mañjīra mañju rasita 
 anuviddha mekhalā kalāpa kala kiṅkiṇī raṇatkāra 
 mukharam parivṛtya bhartṛdārike diṣṭyā vardhāmahe yat atra eva
kaḥ api kasyāpi tiṣṭhati iti mām aṅgulī dala vilāsena
ākhyātavatyaḥ
[P 55]

 Makarandaḥ
svagatam 
hanta mahataḥ prāganurāgasya udbhedaḥ

 Kalahaṃsakaḥ
ākarṇya
 kadhaṃ[katham] sa rasa [sa rasa ] ramaṇīa [ ramaṇīya ]
 aṇubandhiṇī[] itthī [strī ] aṇa [ jana ]
 kadhā[ kathā]

 Makarandaḥ
tataḥ tataḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
 
atrāntare kim api vāgvibhavātivṛtta 

vaicitryam ullasita vibhramam utpalākṣyāḥ
[P 56]
 
tat bhūri sāttvika vikāram apāsta dhairyam
 
ācāryakam vijayi mānmatham āvirāsīt
 tataḥ ca
 
stimita vikasitānām ullasadbhrū latānām
[P 57]
 
masṛṇa mukulitānām prānta vistāra bhājām
 
pratinayana nipāte kiṃ cit ākuñcitānām
 
vividham aham abhūvam pātram ālokitānām
 taiḥ ca
 
a lasa valita mugdha snigdha niṣpanda mandaiḥ
[P 59]
 
adhika vikasadantaḥ vismaya smera tāraiḥ
 
hṛdayam a śaraṇam me pakṣmalākṣyāḥ kaṭākṣaiḥ
 
apahṛtam apaviddham pītam unmūlitam ca
[P 60]
 evam aham tu tasyāḥ sarvākāra hṛdayaṃgamāyāḥ saṃbhāvyamāna 
 sneha rasena abhisaṃdhinā vidheyīkṛtaḥ api pāriplavatvam
ātmanaḥ apanihnotu kāmaḥ prāk prastutasya bakula dāmnaḥ
yathā kathaṃ cit avaśeṣam grathitavān eva
[P 61]
 tataḥ milita vetra śastra pāṇi varṣavara prāya puruṣa 
parivārā gaja vadhūm āruhya nagara gāminam mārgam indu vadanā
alaṃkṛtavatī tadā ca
 
yāntyā muhuḥ valita kandharam ānanam tat
 
āvṛtta vṛnta śatapatra nibham vahantyā
 
digdhaḥ amṛtena ca viṣeṇa ca pakṣmalākṣyā
 
gāḍham nikhātaḥ iva me hṛdaye kaṭākṣaḥ
[P 62]
 tataḥ prabhṛti
 
paricchedātītaḥ sakala vacanānām a viṣayaḥ
 
punaḥ janmani asmin anubhava patham yaḥ na gatavān
 
viveka pradhvaṃsāt upacita mahā moha gahanaḥ
 
vikāraḥ kaḥ api antaḥ jaḍayati ca tāpam ca kurute
[P 63]
 api ca
 
pariccheda vyaktiḥ bhavati na puraḥ sthe api viṣaye
 
bhavati abhyaste api smaraṇam a tathā bhāva vi rasam
 
na santāpa cchedaḥ hima sarasi vā candramasi vā
 
manaḥ niṣṭhā śūnyaṃ bhramati ca kim api ālikhati ca

 Kalahaṃsaḥ
 diḍhaṃ[dṛḍham] khu[khalu] eso[eṣaḥ] kaa vi[kayāpi]

svagatam

iti ādi nanu iha niḥ arthakam eva yasmāt

 prakāśam

 Mādhavaḥ
sakhe śrūyatām atha tasyāḥ kareṇukārohaṇa samaye
[P 65]
 eva mahataḥ sakhī kadambakāt anyatamā vāra yoṣit vilambya
 bāla bakula kusumāvacaya krameṇa nedīyasī bhūtvā
praṇamya kusumāpīḍa vyājena mām evam uktavatī mahā bhāga
su śliṣṭa guṇa tayā ramaṇīyaḥ eṣaḥ vaḥ sumanasām saṃniveśaḥ
kutūhalinī ca naḥ bhartṛdārikā 
[P 66]
 vartate tasyām abhinavaḥ vicitraḥ kusumeṣu vyāpāraḥ[Kusumeṣu 
 vyāpāraḥ] tat bhavatu kṛtārthatā vaidagdhyasya phalatu
nirmāṇa ramaṇīyatā Vidhātuḥ samāsādayatu sa rasaḥ eṣaḥ

 kiṃ ṇāma[kiṃ nāma] Māladī[Mālatī] tti[iti] diṭṭhiā[diṣṭyā]

 Mādhavaḥ

 mukha

svagatam 
 su samīhidaṃ[su samīhitam] khu[khalu] jādaṃ[jātam]
 tataḥ praviśati Kāmandakī Avalokitā ca

 Kāmandakī
sādhu sakhe Bhūrivaso sādhu prabhavati nijasya kanyakā janasya
mahā rājaḥ iti ubhaya lokā viruddham vacanam upanyastam
[P 104]
 api ca adya Manmathodyāna vṛttāntena bhagavataḥ vidheḥ
anukūlatām avagacchāmi bakulāvalī citra phalaka 
 vyatikara saṃvidhāna kautukam adbhutam pramodam ullāsayati
itaretarānurāgaḥ hi dārakarmaṇi parārdhyam maṅgalam gītaḥ ca
ayam arthaḥ Aṅgirasā yasyām vā manaḥ cakṣuṣoḥ
anubandhaḥ tasyām
ṛddhiḥ iti

 Avalokitā
 eṣā[eṣā] Māladī[Mālatī]

 Kāmandakī
nirvarṇya
nikāmam kṣāmāṅgī sa rasa kadalī garbha su bhagā
kalā śeṣā mūrtiḥ śaśinaḥ iva netrotsava karī
[P 105]
avasthām āpannā Madana dahanoddāha vidhurām
iyam naḥ kalyāṇī ramayati manaḥ kampayati ca
 api ca
pari pāṇḍu pāṃsula kapolam ānanam
dadhatī manoharataratvam āgatā
ramaṇīya janmani jane paribhraman
lalitaḥ vidhiḥ vijayate hi mānmathaḥ
 niyatam anayā saṃkalpa nirmitaḥ priya samāgamaḥ anubhūyate
tathā hi asyāḥ
nīvī bandhocchvasanam adhara spandanam doḥ viṣādaḥ
svedaḥ cakṣuḥ masṛṇa madhurākekara snigdha mugdham
[P 106]
gātra stambhaḥ stana mukulayoḥ utprabandhaḥ prakampaḥ
gaṇḍābhoge pulaka paṭalam mūrchanā cetanā ca
 iti upasarpati
[P 107]

 Kāmandakī
ayi sarale kim atra mayā bhagavatyā śakyam prabhavati
[P 112]
 prāyaḥ kumārīṇām janayitā daivam ca yat ca kila Kauśikī Śakuntalā
 Duṣyantam apsarāḥ Purūravasam cakame iti ākhyāna vidaḥ ācakṣate
 Vāsavadattā ca Saṃjayāya rājñe pitrā dattam ātmānam Udayanāya
prāyacchat iti ādi tat api sāhasābhāsam iti an 
 upadeṣṭavyaḥ eva ayam arthaḥ sarvathā
rājñaḥ priyāya suhṛde sacivāya kāryāt
dattvā ātmajām bhavatu nirvṛtimān amātyaḥ

duḥ darśanena ghaṭatām iyam api anena

[P 115]

 Lavaṅgikā
 tadhā[tathā] avi[api] ācakkhadu[ācaṣṭām] bhaavadī[bhagavatī]
 pasādaṃ[prasādam] karedu[karotu]

 Kāmandakī
śrūyatām asti Vidarbhādhipasya narapateḥ amātyaḥ samagra 
 dhurya puruṣa prakāṇḍa cakra cūḍā maṇiḥ Devarātaḥ nāma
yam a śeṣa bhuvana mahanīya puṇya mahimānam ātmanaḥ
satīrthyam pitā eva te jānāti yaḥ asau yādṛśaḥ ca iti api ca
vyatikarita digantāḥ śvetamānaiḥ yaśobhiḥ
su kṛta vilasitānām sthānam ūrjasvalānām
[P 116]
a kalita mahimānaḥ ketanam maṅgalānām
katham api bhuvane asmin tādṛśāḥ saṃbhavanti

 Mālatī
janāntikam 
 sahi[sakhi] taṃ[tam] khu[khalu] bhaavadī [bhagavatī ]
 gahida [ gṛhīta ] ṇāmaheaṃ[ nāmadheyam] savvadā[sarvadā]
 tādo[tātaḥ] sumaredi[smarati] 

 Lavaṅgikā
 sahi[sakhi] samaṃ[samam] kila[kila] vijjā [vidyā ]
 ahigamo[ adhigamaḥ] kido[kṛtaḥ] tti[iti] tak [tat ]
 kāla [ kāla ] vediṇo[ vedinaḥ] mantaanti[mantrayante]
[P 117]

 Kāmandakī
tataḥ udaya gireḥ iva ekaḥ eva
sphurita guṇa dyuti sundaraḥ kalāvān
iha jagati mahotsavasya hetuḥ
nayanavatām udiyāya bāla candraḥ //10//

 Lavaṅgikā
janāntikam 
 sahi[sakhi] avi ṇāma[api nāma] Māhavo[Mādhavaḥ]
 bhave[bhavet]

 Kāmandakī
asau vidyādhāraḥ śiśuḥ api vinirgatya bhavanāt
iha āyātaḥ saṃprati a vikala śaraccandra madhuraḥ
[P 118]
yad āloka sthāne bhavati puram unmāda taralaiḥ
kaṭākṣaiḥ nārīṇām kuvalayita vātāyanam iva
 atra bāla suhṛdā Makarandena saha vidyām ānvīkṣikīm
adhigacchati eṣaḥ Mādhavaḥ nāma

 Mālatī
sānandam janāntikam 
 sahi[sakhi] sudaṃ[śrutam] tue[tvayā]

 Lavaṅgikā
 sahi[sakhi] kudo[kutaḥ] vā[vā] mahodahiṃ[mahodadhim]
 vajjia[varjayitvā] pārijādassa[pāūrijātasya] uggamo[udgamaḥ]
 tti[iti]
[P 119]
 nepathye śaṅkha dhvaniḥ

 Kāmandakī aho kālātipātaḥ saṃprati hi
kṣipan nidrā mudrām madana kalaha ccheda su labhām
avāptotkaṇṭhānām vihaga mithunānām prathamataḥ
dadhānaḥ saudhānām a laghuṣu nikuñjeṣu ghanatām
asau saṃdhyā śaṅkha dhvaniḥ a nibhṛtaḥ khe vicarati
 vatse sukham sthīyatām
iti uttiṣṭhati
[P 120]

apavārya 
 kadhaṃ[katham] uvahārīkidā[upahārīkṛtā] mhi[asmi]

sānandam 
 kadhaṃ[katham] mahā kula [mahā kula ]
 pabhavo[ prabhavaḥ] vi[api] so[saḥ]

 Lavaṅgikā
 Avaloãde[Avalokite] ido[itaḥ] ido[itaḥ] ediṇā[etena]
 sovāṇeṇa[sopānena] odaramha[avatarāvaḥ]

 Kāmandakī
apavārya 
sādhu saṃprati mayā taṭasthayā eva Mālatīm prati nisṛṣṭārtha dūtī vṛttasya laghūkṛtaḥ bhāraḥ tathā hi
vare anyasmin dveṣaḥ pitari vicikitsā ca janitā
purā vṛttodgāraiḥ api ca kathitā kārya padavī
[P 122]
stutam māhā bhāgyam yat abhijanataḥ yat ca guṇataḥ
prasaṅgāt vatsasya iti atha khalu vidheyaḥ paricayaḥ
 iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve
 Dhavala gṛhaḥ nāma dvitīyaḥ aṅkaḥ
[P 123]
 tṛtīyaḥ aṅkaḥ
tataḥ praviśati Buddharakṣitā

 Kāmandakī
tathā vinaya namrā api 
mayā Mālatī upāyataḥ
nītā katipayāhena 
sakhī visrambha sevyatām
 saṃprati hi
vrajati virahe vaicittyam naḥ prasīdati saṃnidhau
rahasi ramate prītyā vācam dadūāti anuvartate
[P 129]
gamana samaye kaṇṭhe lagnā nirudhya nirudhya mām
sapadi śapathaiḥ pratyāvṛttim praṇamya ca yācate
 Idam ca tatra sādhīyaḥ pratyāśā nibandhanam
Śākuntalādīn itihāsa vādān
prastāvitān anyaparaiḥ vacobhiḥ
śrutvā madutsaṅga niveśitāṅgī
 
cirāya cintā stimitatvam eti
 tat adya Mādhava samakṣam uttaram upakramiṣye
 nepathyābhimukham
vatse itaḥ itaḥ
[P 130]
 tataḥ praviśati Mālatī Lavaṅgikā ca

 Mālatī kadhaṃ[katham] uvahārīkida[upahārīkṛtā]
 mhi[asmi] iti ādi pūrvoktam paṭhati

 Lavaṅgikā
 sahi[sakhi] eso[eṣaḥ] khu[khalu] mahura [madhura ]
 mahu [ madhu ] rasa [ rasa ] ulla [ ārdra ]
 ulla [ ārdra ] mañjarī [ mañjarī ] kavalaṇa [ kavalana ]

 suhābinduãjjanta [ sudhābindūyamāna ] muddha [ mugdha ]

 candaṇāamāṇa [ candanāyamāna ] sīala [ śītala ]
 phaṃso[ sparśaḥ] tumaṃ[tvām] parissaadi[pariṣvajati]
 Kusumāarojjāṇa [Kusumākarodyāna ] mārudo[ mārutaḥ]

parikramya praviśataḥ
[P 132]
 tataḥ praviśati Mādhavaḥ
 Mādhavaḥ
sa harṣam
hanta parāgatā bhagavatī iyam hi mama
āvirbhavantī prathamam priyāyāḥ 
socchvāsam antaḥkaraṇam karoti
saṃtāpa dagdhasya śikhaṇḍi yūnaḥ 
vṛṣṭeḥ purastāt
 a cira prabhā iva
 diṣṭyā Lavaṅgikā dvitīyā Mālatī api
āścaryam utpala dṛśo vadanāmalendu 
 sāṃnidhyataḥ mama muhuḥ jaḍimānam etya
[P 133]
jātyena candramaṇinā iva mahīdharasya
saṃdhāryate dravamayaḥ manasā vikāraḥ
 saṃprati ramaṇīyatarā Mālatī
jvalayati manobhavāgnim madayati hṛdayam kṛtārthayati cakṣuḥ
[P 134]
parimṛdita campakāvali vilāsa lulitālasaiḥ aṅgaiḥ

 Mālatī
 sahi[sakhi] imassiṃ[asmin] kujjaa [kubjaka ]
 ṇiuñje[ nikuñje] kusumāãṃ[kusumāni] avaciṇumha[avacinuvaḥ]
 Mādhavaḥ
prathama priyā vacana saṃśrava sphurat 
 pulakena saṃprati mayā viḍambyate
[P 135]
ghana rāji nūtana payaḥ samukṣaṇa 
 kṣaṇa baddha kuṭmala kadamba ḍambaraḥ

 Lavaṅgikā
 sahi[sakhi] evaṃ[evam] karemha[kurvaḥ]
puṣpāvacayam nāṭayataḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
a parimeyāścaryam ācāryakam bhagavatyāḥ

 Mālatī
 ido[itaḥ] vi[api] aṇṇassiṃ[anyasmin] avaciṇumha[avacinuvaḥ]

 Kāmandakī
Mālatīm pariṣvajya 
ayi virama niḥ sahā asi jātā
[P 136]
 tathā hi
skhalayati vacanam te sraṃsayati aṅgam aṅgam
janayati mukha candrodbhāsinaḥ sveda bindūn
mukulayati ca netre sarvathā subhru khedaḥ
tvayi vilasati tulyam vallabhālokanena
 Mālatī lajjate
 Lavaṅgikā
 sohaṇaṃ[śobhanam] bhaavadīe[bhagavatyā] āṇattaṃ[ājñaptam]
[P 137]

 Mādhavaḥ
hṛdayaṃgamaḥ parihāsaḥ

 Kāmandakī
tat āsyatām kiṃ cit ākhyeyam ākhyātu kāmā asmi
 sarvāḥ upaviśanti

 Kāmandakī
Mālatyāḥ cibukam unnamayya 
śṛṇu vicitram idam subhage

 Mālatī
 avahidā[avahitā] mhi[asmi]

 Kāmandakī
asti tāvat ekadā prasaṅgataḥ kathitaḥ eva mayā Mādhava 
 abhidhānaḥ kumāraḥ yaḥ tvam iva māmakīnasya manasaḥ dvitīyam
 nibandhanam

 Lavaṅgikā
 sumarāmo[smarāmaḥ]

 Kāmandakī
saḥ khalu Manmathodyāna yātrā divasāt prabhṛti
durmanāyamānaḥ paravān iva śarīropatāpena
yat indau ānandam praṇayini jane vā na bhajate
vyanakti antaḥ tāpam tat ayam ati dhīraḥ api viṣamam
[P 138]
priyaṅgu śyāmāṅga prakṛtiḥ api ca āpāṇḍu madhuram
vapuḥ kṣāmam kṣāmam vahati ramaṇīyaḥ ca bhavati
 Lavaṅgikā
 edaṃ[etat] pi[api] tassiṃ[tasmin] avasare[avasare]
 bhaavadiṃ[bhagavatīm] tuvarāvantīe[tvarayantyā]
 Avaloãdāe[Avalokitayā] udīridaṃ[udīritam] āsī[āsīt]
 a sattha [ a svastha ] sarīro[ śarīraḥ] Māhavo[Mādhavaḥ]
 tti[iti]

 Kāmandakī
yāvat aśṛṇavam Mālatī eva asya Manmathonmāda 
 hetuḥ iti mama
api saḥ eva niścayaḥ
[P 139]
anubhavam vadanenduḥ upāgamat
niyatam eṣaḥ yat asya mahātmanaḥ
kṣubhitam utkalikā taralam manaḥ
payaḥ iva stimitasya mahodadheḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
aho upanyāsa śuddhiḥ aho mahattvāropaṇe yatnaḥ athavā
śāstreṣu niṣṭhā sahajaḥ ca bodhaḥ
prāgalbhyam abhyasta guṇā ca vāṇī
kālānurodhaḥ pratibhānavattvam
ete guṇāḥ kāma dughāḥ kriyāsu
[P 140]
 Kāmandakī
ataḥ tena jīvitāt udvijamānena duḥ karam api na kiṃ cit na
kriyate asau hi
dhatte cakṣuḥ mukulini raṇat kokile bāla cūte
mārge gātram kṣipati bakulāmoda garbhasya vāyoḥ
dāha premṇā sa rasa bisinī pattra mātrāntarāyaḥ
tāmyanmūrtiḥ śrayati bahuśaḥ mṛtyave candra pādān
[P 141]

 Mādhavaḥ
anyaḥ eva a kṣuṇṇaḥ kathā prakāraḥ bhagavatyāḥ

svagatam 
 adi duk karaṃ[ati duḥ karam] karedi[karoti]

 vaasso[ vayasyaḥ] paḍivaṇṇa [pratipanna ] ceaṇo[ cetanaḥ]

 Mādhavaḥ
āśvasya 
vayasya sāhasika ehi ehi 
iti āliṅgati

 Kāmandakī
ubhau śirasi āghrāya
diṣṭyā jīvita vatsā asmi

 itarāḥ
 piaṃ[priyam] piaṃ[priyam] ṇo[naḥ] saṃvuttaṃ[saṃvṛttam]
sarvāḥ harṣam nāṭayanti

 Buddharakṣitā
janāntikam 
 halā[sakhi] Madaantie[Madayantike] eso[eṣaḥ] jevva[eva]
 so[saḥ]

 Madayantikā
 jāṇidaṃ[jñātam] jevva[eva] mae[mayā] jahā[yathā] esa[eṣaḥ]
 Māhavo[Mādhavaḥ] aaṃ[ayam] pi[api] so[saḥ] jaṇo[janaḥ]
 tti[iti]

 Buddharakṣitā
 avi[api] sacca [satya ] vādiṇī[ vādinī] ahaṃ[aham]

 Madayantikā
 ṇa[na] hu[khalu] aṇṇārisesu[anyādṛśeṣu]
 tumhārisīo[yuṣmādṛśyaḥ] pakkhavādiṇīo[pakṣapātinyaḥ] 
[P 173]
 honti[bhavanti]
Mādhavam avalokya 
 sahi[sakhi] Māladīe[Mālatyāḥ] vi[api] ramaṇijjo[ramaṇīyaḥ]
 imassiṃ[asmin] mahāṇuhāve[mahānubhāve]
 aṇurāa [anurāga ] pavādo[ pravādaḥ]
 punaḥ Makarandam eva sa spṛham avalokayati
 Kāmandakī
svagatam 
ramaṇīyorjitam hi Madayantikā Makarandayoḥ daivāt
 adya
darśanam 
prakāśam 
vatsa Makaranda katham punaḥ āyuṣmān asmin avasare Madayantikā 
 jīvita trāṇa hetuḥ bhagavatā daivena saṃnidhāpitaḥ
[P 174]

 Makarandaḥ
adya aham antaḥ nagare eva kāṃ cit vārtām upaśrutya Mādhavasya
 cittodvegam adhikam āśaṅkamānaḥ tvaritam Avalokitā 
 nivedita Kusumākarodyāna vṛttāntaḥ parāpatan eva
śārdūlāvaskanda gocara gatām etām abhijāta kanyakām
abhyupapannavān asmi
Mālatī Mādhavau vimṛśataḥ

 Kāmandakī
svagatam
 vṛttena khalu Mālatī pradānena bhavitavyam 
 prakāśam vatsa Mādhava diṣṭyā suhṛdbuddhyā vardhitaḥ asi
Mālatyā tat ayam avasaraḥ prīti dāyasya

 Mādhavaḥ

[P 175]
tat kāmam prabhavati pūrṇa pātra vṛttyā
svīkartum mama hṛdayaṃ ca jīvitam ca

 Lavaṅgikā
 paḍicchido[pratīṣṭaḥ] khu[khalu] ṇo[naḥ] pia [priya ]

 kiṃ ṇāma[kiṃ nāma] Maarandeṇa[Makarandena] uvvea [udvega ]

 praviśya

 jaã[yadi] devvam[daivam] aṇuūlaṃ[anukūlam]
 bhavissadi[bhaviṣyati]

 Mādhavaḥ
apavārya
cirāt āśā tantuḥ truṭatu bisinī sūtra bhiduraḥ
mahān ādhi vyādhiḥ niḥ avadhiḥ idānīm prasaratu
[P 182]
pratiṣṭhām a vyājam vrajatu mayi pāriplava dhurā
vidhiḥ svāsthyam dhattām bhavatu kṛta kṛtyaḥ ca Madanaḥ
 athavā
samāna premāṇam janam a sulabham prārthitavataḥ
vidhau vāmārambhe mama samucitā eṣā pariṇatiḥ
tathāpi asmin dāna śravaṇa samaye asyāḥ pravigalat 

prabham prātaḥ candra dyuti vadanam antaḥ dahati mām

 Kāmandakī
svagatam 
evam ati durmanāyamānaḥ pīḍayati mām vatsaḥ Mādhavaḥ vatsā

 prakāśam

sa lajjam
nahi nahi

 Kāmandakī
jānāmi tām khalu vārtām idam tu prasiddham eva yathā Nandanāya
Mālatīm prārthayamānaṃ Bhūrivasuḥ nṛpatim uktavān prabhavati
 nijasya kanyakā janasya mahā rāja iti

 Makarandaḥ
asti etat

 Kāmandakī
adya ca svayam rājñā eva dattā Mālatī iti puruṣeṇa āveditam tat
vatsa vāk pratiṣṭhāni dehinām vyavahāra tantrāṇi vāci puṇya 
 a puṇya hetavaḥ vyavasthāḥ sarveṣām janānām āyatante iti sā
 ca Bhūrivasoḥ vāk anṛtātmikā eva na khalu mahā rājasya
Mālatī nijā kanyakā kanyā dāne ca nṛpatayaḥ pramāṇam iti na
evaṃvidhaḥ dharmācāra samayaḥ tasmāt a vimarśitavyam etat
katham ca vatsa mām annavadhānām manyase
[P 185]
 paśya
mā vā sapatneṣu api nāma tat bhūt
pāpam yat asyām tvayi vā viśaṅkyam
tat sarvathā saṃgamanāya yatnaḥ
prāṇa vyayena api mayā vidheyaḥ

 Makarandaḥ
suṣṭhu yujyamānam ādiśyate yuṣmābhiḥ api ca
dayā vā snehaḥ vā bhagavati nije asmin śiśu jane

[P 186]

prasaktaḥ te yatnaḥ prabhavati punaḥ daivam aparam

 nepathye

 mahā bhāa[mahā bhāga] loaṇa [locana ] āṇanda[ ānanda]

nūnam āśvāsana mātram etat Mādhavasya sahaja sneha 
 kātarā

sodvegam

 Makarandaḥ
atha kim
tat me manaḥ kṣipati yat sa rasa prahāram
ālokya mām a gaṇita skhaladuttarīyā
trastaika hāyana kuraṅga vilola dṛṣṭiḥ
āśliṣṭavatī amṛta saṃvalitaiḥ iva aṅgaiḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
su labhā eva Buddharakṣitā priya sakhī bhavataḥ api ca
pramathya kravyādaṃ maraṇa samaye rakṣitavataḥ
pariṣvaṅgam labdhvā tava katham iva anyatra ramatām
[P 191]
tathā ca vyāpāraḥ kamala nayanāyāḥ nayanayoḥ
tvayi vyakta sneha stimita ramaṇīyaḥ ciram abhūt

 Makarandaḥ
tat uttiṣṭha Pārā Sindhu saṃbhedam avagāhya nagarīm eva
praviśāvaḥ 
 utthāya parikrāmataḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
ayam asau mahā nadyoḥ saṃbhedaḥ yaḥ eṣaḥ
jala nibiḍita vastra vyakta nimnonnatābhiḥ
parigata taṭa bhūmiḥ snāna mātrotthitābhiḥ
[P 192]
rucira kanaka kumbha śrīmadābhoga tuṅga 
 stana vinihita hasta svastikābhiḥ vadhūbhiḥ
 iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve
 Śārdūla vibhramaḥ nāma caturthaḥ aṅkaḥ

[P 193]
 pañcamaḥ aṅkaḥ
tataḥ praviśati ākāśa yānena bhīṣaṇojjvala veśā
Kapālakuṇḍalā

 Kapālakuṇḍalā
ṣaḍadhika daśa nāḍī cakra madhya sthitātmā 
hṛdi vinihita rūpaḥ siddhi daḥ tadvidām yaḥ
[P 194]
a vicalita manobhiḥ sādhakaiḥ mṛgyamāṇaḥ
saḥ jayati pariṇaddhaḥ śaktibhiḥ Śakti nāthaḥ
[P 195]
 iyam aham idānīm
nityam nyasta ṣaḍaṅga cakra nihitam hṛt padma 
 madhyoditam
paśyantī Śiva rūpiṇam laya vaśāṭ ātmānam abhyāgatā
[P 196]
nāḍīnām udaya krameṇa jagataḥ pañcāmṛtākarṣaṇāt
a prāptotpatana śramā vighaṭayantī agra nabhaḥ ambhaḥ mucaḥ
[P 197]
 api ca
ullola skhalita kapāla kaṇṭha mālā 
 saṃghaṭṭa kvaṇita karāla kiṅkiṇīkaḥ
paryāptam mayi ramaṇīya ḍāmaratvam
saṃdhatte gagana tala prayāṇa vegaḥ
 tathā hi
viṣvagvṛttiḥ jaṭānām pracalati nibiḍa granthi baddhaḥ api
bhāraḥ
saṃskāra kvāṇa dīrgham paṭu raṭati kṛtāvṛtti khaṭvāṅga 
 ghaṇṭā
[P 198]
ūrdhvam dhūnoti vāyuḥ vivṛta śava śiraḥ śreṇi kuñjeṣu
guñjan
uttālaḥ kiṃkiṇīnām annavarata raṇatkāra hetuḥ patākāḥ
 parikramya avalokya ca gandham āghrāya
idam tāvat purāṇa nimba tailākta paribhṛjyamāna 
 rasona gandhibhiḥ citā dhūmaiḥ purastāt vibhāvitasya mahataḥ 
[P 199]
 śmaśāna vāṭasya nedīyaḥ Karālāyatanam yatra paryavasita 
 mantra sādhanasya asmadguroḥ Aghoraghaṇṭasya
ājñayā sa viśeṣam adya pūjā saṃbhāraḥ mayā saṃnidhāpanīyaḥ kathitam ca
me guruṇā vatse Kapālakuṇḍale adya bhagavatyāḥ Karālāyāḥ prāk
upayācitam strī ratnam upahartavyam tat atra eva nagare viditam
āste iti tat vicinomi 
sa kautukam avalokya
 tat kaḥ ayam ati gambhīra madhurākṛtiḥ uttambhita 
 kuṭila kuntalaḥ kṛpāṇa pāṇiḥ śmaśāna vāṭam avatarati yaḥ
eṣaḥ
[P 200]
kuvalaya dala śyāmaḥ api aṅgam dadhat pari dhūsaram
lalita vikaṭa nyāsaḥ śrīmān mṛgāṅka nibhānanaḥ
harati vinayam vāmaḥ yasya prakāśita sāhasaḥ
pravigaladasṛk paṅkaḥ pāṇiḥ lalannara jāṅgalaḥ
[P 201]
 nirūpya
aye saḥ eṣaḥ Kāmandakī suhṛt putraḥ mahā māṃsasya
 paṇāyitā
Mādhavaḥ tat kim anena bhavatu samīhitam saṃpādayāmi vigalita 
 prāyaḥ paścima saṃdhyā samayaḥ tathā hi saṃprati
vyomnaḥ tāpiccha gucchāvalibhiḥ iva tamaḥ vallarībhiḥ
vriyante
paryantāḥ prānta vṛttyā payasi vasumatī nūtane majjati iva
vātyā saṃvega viṣvagvitata valayita sphīta dhūmyā 
 prakāśam
prārambhe api triyāmā taruṇayati nijam nīlimānaṃ vaneṣu
[P 202]
 parikramya niṣkrāntā
viṣkambhakaḥ
 tataḥ praviśati yathā nirdiṣṭaḥ Mādhavaḥ
 Mādhavaḥ
sāśaṃsam
premārdrāḥ praṇaya spṛśaḥ paricayāt udgāḍha rāgodayāḥ
tāḥ tāḥ mugdha dṛśaḥ nisarga madhurāḥ ceṣṭāḥ bhaveyuḥ mayi
yāsu antaḥkaraṇasya bāhya karaṇa vyāpāra rodhī kṣaṇāt
āśaṃsā parikalpitāsu api bhavati ānanda sāndraḥ layaḥ
[P 203]
 kiṃ ca
atimukta madgrathita kesarāvalī 
 satatādhivāsa subhagārpita stanam
api karṇajāha viniveśitānanam
priyayā tadaṅga parivartam āpnuyām
 athavā dūre tāvat etat idam eva prārthaye
[P 204]
saṃbhūya iva sukhāni cetasi param bhūmānam ātanvate
yatra āloka pathāvatāriṇi ratim prastauti netrotsavaḥ
yat bālendu kaloccayāt avacitaiḥ sāraiḥ iva utpāditam
tat paśyeyam Annaṅga maṅgala gṛham bhūyaḥ api tasyāḥ mukham
[P 205]
 yat satyam adhunā tat saṃdarśanena ati svalpaḥ api na viśeṣaḥ
mama hi saṃprati sātiśaya prāktanopalambha saṃbhāvita 
 ātma janmanaḥ saṃskārasya annavarata prabodhāt pratāyamānaḥ
tadvisadṛśa pratyayāntaraiḥ a tiraskṛta pravāhaḥ
 priyatamā smṛti pratyayotpatti saṃtānaḥ tanmayam iva
karoti antaḥ vṛtti sārūpyataḥ caitanyam tathā hi
[P 206]
linā iva pratibimbitā iva likhitā iva utkīrṇa rūpā iva ca
pratyuptā iva ca vajra lepa ghaṭitā iva antaḥ nikhātā iva
ca
[P 207]
sā naḥ cetasi kīlitā iva viśikhaiḥ Cetaḥ bhuvaḥ pañcabhiḥ
cintā saṃtati tantu jāla nibiḍa syūtā iva lagnā priyā
[P 208]
 nepathye kalakalaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
aho saṃprati pragalbhamāna kauṇapa nikāyasya mahatī
 śmaśāna vāṭasya raudratā asmin hi
paryanta pratirodhi medura caya styānam citā jyotiṣām
aujjvalyam para bhāgataḥ prakaṭayati ābhoga bhīmaṃ tamaḥ

tasyāḥ cibukam unnamayya 
vatse

vidhātuḥ vaidagdhyam vilasatu sa kāmaḥ astu Madanaḥ

 Makarandaḥ
rambhoru saṃhara bhayam kṣamate visoḍhum
utkampitam stana bharasya na madhya bhāgaḥ
ittham tvayā eva kathita praṇaya prasādaḥ
saṃkalpa nirvṛtiṣu saṃstutaḥ eṣaḥ dāsaḥ
 Buddharakṣitā

preyān manoratha sahasra vṛtaḥ saḥ eṣaḥ
supta pramatta janam etat amātya veśma
[P 330]

iti ādi paṭhati Madayantikā aśrūṇi pātayati

 Mādhavaḥ
sānandam

tathā hi

timira nikaram udyan aindavaḥ prāk prakāśaḥ
viyati pavana vegāt unmukhaḥ ketakīnām
pracalitaḥ iva sāndraḥ mākarandaḥ parāgaḥ
[P 336]
 svagatam
tat katham vāma śīlām Mālatīm upāvartaye bhavatu evam tāvat
 prakāśam
priye Mālati pratyagra sāyaṃtana snāna sa viśeṣa 
 śītalām atrabhavatīm nidāgha saṃtāpa śāntaye vijñāpayāmi tat
kim iti adya a nimittam anyathā eva saṃbhāvayasi
niścotante su tanu kabarī bindavaḥ yāvat ete
yāvat madhyaḥ stana mukulayoḥ na ārdra bhāvaṃ jahāti
yāvat sāndra pratanu pulakodbhedavatī aṅga yaṣṭiḥ
tāvat gāḍham vitara sakṛt api aṅkapālīm prasīda
[P 337]
 ayi niḥ anurodhe
jīvayan iva samūḍha sādhvasa 
 sveda binduḥ adhi kaṇṭham arpyatām
bāhuḥ aindava mayūkha cumbita 
 syandi candramaṇi hāra vibhramaḥ
 athavā dūre tāvat etat katham ālāpa saṃvibhāgasya api
a bhājanam ayam janaḥ bhavatyāḥ
dagdham cirāya Malayānila candra pādaiḥ
nirvāpitam tu parirabhya vapuḥ na nāma
[P 338]
āmatta kokila ruta vyathitā tu hṛdyām
adya śrutiḥ pibatu kiṃnara kaṇṭhi vācam

 Saudāminī

Padmāvatī vimala vāri viśāla Sindhu 
 Pārā sarit parikara cchalataḥ bibharti

 api ca

 anyataḥ avalokya
ayam asau bhagavatyāḥ Sindhoḥ dārita rasātalaḥ taṭa prapātaḥ

 gambhīra nūtana ghana stanita pracaṇḍaḥ
paryanta bhūdhara nikuñja vijṛmbhamāṇaḥ
Heramba kaṇṭha rasita pratimānam eti

 etāḥ ca candanāśvakarṇa kesara pāṭalā prāya taru

smārayanti khalu taruṇa kadamba jambū vanāvanaddha

 pratiṣṭhaḥ Suvarṇabinduḥ iti ākhyāyate
praṇamya

 guro

 Makarandaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
hā priye Mālati kva asi katham a vijñāta tattvam adbhutam
 jhaṭiti paryavasitā asi nanu a karuṇe prasīda saṃbhāvaya mām

[P 380]
priya mādhave kim asi mayi a vatsalā
nanu saḥ aham eva yam anandayat purā

 vayasya Makaranda duḥ labhaḥ punaḥ jagati tāvataḥ snehasya
saṃbhavaḥ 

sa rasa kusuma kṣāmaiḥ aṅgaiḥ anaṅga mahā jvaraḥ
ciram a viratonmāthī soḍhaḥ pratikṣaṇa dāruṇaḥ
[P 381]

 api ca
mayi vigalita pratyāśatvāt vivāha vidheḥ purā
vikala karuṇaiḥ marma ccheda vyathā vidhuraiḥ iva
smarasi ruditaiḥ snehākūtam tathā vyatanot asau
aham api yathā abhūvam pīḍā taraṃgita mānasaḥ

 sāvegam
aha nu khalu bhoḥ

 drumāḥ

āvirbhūta śilīndhra lodhra kusuma smerā vanānām tatiḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
sakhe paśyāmi kiṃ tu duḥ āloka ramaṇīyāḥ saṃprati
 araṇya 
 giri bhūmayaḥ tat kim etat 
sāsram
athavā kim anyat

utphullārjuna sarja vāsita vahat paurastya 
 jhañjhāmarut 
 preṅkhola skhalitendranīla śakala snigdhāmbuda 
 śreṇayaḥ
[P 388]
dhārā sikta vasuṃdharā surabhayaḥ prāptāḥ te ete adhunā
gharmāmbhaḥ vigamāgama vyatikara śrī vāhinaḥ
vāsarāḥ

 hā priye Mālati

taruṇa tamāla nīla bahulonnamadambudharāḥ
śiśira samīraṇāvadhūta nūtana vāri kaṇāḥ
[P 389]
katham avalokayeyam adhunā harihetimatīḥ
mada kala nīlakaṇṭha kalahaiḥ mukharāḥ kakubhaḥ

 śokārtim nāṭayati

 Makarandaḥ
kaḥ api ati dāruṇaḥ daśā vipākaḥ saṃprati vayasyasya
 sāsram
mayā punaḥ vajrāyamāṇena vinodaḥ ārabdhaḥ 
niḥśvasya

[P 390]
 evam ca paryavasita prāyā eva naḥ Mādhava pratyāśā
sa bhayam vilokya
 katham pramugdhaḥ eva
ākāśe
Mālati Mālati kim aparam niḥ anukrośā asi

apahastita bāndhave tvayā 
vihitam sāhasam asya tṛṣṇayā
tat iha annaparādhini priye
sakhi kaḥ ayam karuṇojjhita kramaḥ

 katham adya api na ucchvasiti hanta muṣitaḥ asmi daivena

mātar mātar dalati hṛdayam dhvaṃsate deha bandhaḥ
śūnyam manye jagat a virata jvālam antaḥ jvalāmi
sīdan andhe tamasi vidhuraḥ majjati iva antaḥ ātmā
[P 391]
viṣvak mohaḥ sthagayati katham manda bhāgyaḥ karomi 

 kaṣṭaṃ bhoḥ kaṣṭam

bandhutā hṛdaya kaumudī mahaḥ
Mālatī nayana mugdha candramāḥ
saḥ ayam adya Makaranda nandanaḥ 
jīva loka tilakaḥ pralīyate

 hā vayasya Mādhava

gātreṣu candana rasaḥ dṛśi śāradenduḥ
ānandaḥ eva hṛdaye mama yaḥ tvam āsīḥ
tam tvām nikāma kamanīyam akāṇḍe eva
kālena jīvitam iva uddharatā hataḥ asmi

 spṛśan

[P 392]
a karuṇa vitara smitojjvalām
dṛśam ati dāruṇa dehi me giram
sahacaram anurakta cetasam
priya Makaranda katham na manyase

 Mādhavaḥ saṃjñām labhate

 Makarandaḥ
socchvāsam
ayam a cira dhauta rājapaṭṭa rucira māṃsala 
 chaviḥ
nava jaladharaḥ toya sīkarāsāreṇa priya vayasyam me
jīvayati diṣṭyā samucchvasitaḥ tāvat

[P 393]
 Mādhavaḥ
tat kam atra vipine priyā vārtā haram karomi 
vilokya
 sādhu sādhu

phala bhara pariṇāma śyāma jambū nikuñja 
 skhalita tanu taraṅgām uttareṇa sravantīm
upari vighaṭamānaḥ prauḍha tāpiccha nīlaḥ
śrayati śikharam adreḥ nūtanaḥ toyavāhaḥ

 sa rabhasam utthāya unmukhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ

[P 394]
kac cit saumya priya sahacarī vidyut āliṅgati tvām
āvirbhūta praṇaya su mukhāḥ cātakāḥ vā bhajante
paurastyaḥ vā sukhayati marut sādhu saṃvāhanābhiḥ
viṣvak bibhrat surapatidhanuḥ lakṣma lakṣmīm tanoti

[P 395]
 ākarṇya
ayam pratirava bharita kandarānanditotkaṇṭha 
 nīlakaṇṭha kala kekānubandhinā mandra huṃkṛtena mām
anumanyate yāvat abhyarthaye bhagavan jīmūta

daivāt paśyeḥ jagati vicaran icchayā mat priyām cet
āśvāsya ādau tat anu kathayeḥ mādhavīyām avasthām
[P 396]
āśā tantuḥ na ca kathayatā atyantam ucchedanīyaḥ
prāṇa trāṇam katham api karoti āyatākṣyāḥ saḥ ekaḥ

 sa harṣam
aye pracalitaḥ tat anyataḥ saṃcarāmi 
parikrāmati 

 Makarandaḥ
sākūtam
katham idānīm unmādoparāgaḥ Mādhavendum āskandati hā tāta
hā amba hā bhagavati paritrāyasva paritrāyasva paśya Mādhavasya
avasthām 

[P 397]
 Mādhavaḥ
dhik pramādaḥ 

naveṣu lodhra prasaveṣu kāntiḥ
dṛśaḥ kuraṅgīṣu gatam gajeṣu
latāsu namratvam iti pramathya
vyaktam vibhaktā vipine priyā me

 Makarandaḥ

suhṛdi guṇa nivāse preyasi prāṇa nāthe
katham api saha pāṃsu krīḍana prauḍha sakhye /
[P 398]
priya jana virahādhi vyādhi vegam dadhāne
hata hṛdaya vidīrya tvam dvidhā na prayāsi

 Mādhavaḥ
su labhānukāraḥ khalu jagati vedhasaḥ nirmāṇa saṃniveśaḥ
 bhavatu evam tāvat
uccaiḥ 
ayam aham bhoḥ praṇipatya bhūdharāraṇya cāriṇaḥ sattvān
vijñāpayāmi mām muhūrtam avadhāna dānena anugṛhṇantu bhavantaḥ

bhavadbhiḥ sarvāṅga prakṛti ramaṇīyā kula vadhūḥ
iha sthaiḥ dṛṣṭā vā viditam athavā asyāḥ kim abhavat
[P 399]
vayaḥ avasthām tasyāḥ śṛṇuta suhṛdaḥ yatra Madanaḥ
pragalbha vyāpāraḥ carati hṛdi mugdhaḥ ca vapuṣi

 kaṣṭaṃ bhoḥ kaṣṭam

kekābhiḥ nīlakaṇṭhaḥ tirayati vacanam tāṇḍavāt ucchikhaṇḍaḥ
kāntām antaḥ pramodāt abhisarati mada bhrānta tāraḥ cakoraḥ
golāṅgūlaḥ kapolam churayati rajasā kausumena priyāyāḥ
kam yāce yatra tatra dhruvam annavasara grastaḥ eva arthi 
 bhāvaḥ

[P 400]
 ayam ca

dantachadāruṇima rañjita kānta dantam
unnamya cumbati valīvadanaḥ priyāyāḥ
kāmpillaka prasava pāṭala gaṇḍa pāli
pākāruṇa sphuṭita dāḍima kānti vaktram

 eṣaḥ priyatamā skandha viśrānta karaḥ karī katham atra api
annavasaraḥ

[P 401]
kaṇḍū kuḍmalitekṣaṇām sahacarīm dantasya koṭyā likhan
paryāya vyatikīrṇa karṇa pavanaiḥ āhlādibhiḥ vījayan
jagdhārdhaiḥ nava sallakī kisalayaiḥ asyāḥ sthitim
kalpayan
dhanyaḥ vanya mataṅgajaḥ paricaya prāgalbhyam abhyasyati

 anyataḥ avalokya ayam tu

na antaḥ vartayati dhvanatsu jaladeṣu āmandram udgarjitam
na āsannāt sarasaḥ karoti kavalān āvarjitaiḥ śaivalaiḥ
[P 402]
dāna jyāni viṣāda mūka madhupa vyāsaṅga dīnānanaḥ
nūnam prāṇa samā viyoga vidhuraḥ stamberamaḥ tāmyati

 alam anena āyāsitena eṣaḥ sānanda sahacarī 
 samākarṇyamāna madhura gambhīra kaṇṭha garjita dhvaniḥ
aparaḥ matta mātaṅga yūtha pālaḥ pratyagra vikasita 
[P 403]
 kadamba saṃghāta surabhi śītalāmoda bahala 
 saṃgalita māṃsala kapola niṣyanda kardamita karaṭaḥ
samuddalita kamalinī khaṇḍa viprakīrṇa parṇa kamala 
 kesara mṛṇāla bisa kanda komalāṅkura nikaram an 
 avarata pravṛtta kamanīya karṇa tāla tāṇḍava 
 pracala jarjarita jala taraṃga vitata nīhāram
uttrasta kurara sārasam saraḥ avagāhya viharati bhavatu
ābhāṣe mahā bhāga nāga pate ślāghya yauvanaḥ khalu asi
kāntānuvṛtti cāturyam api asti bhavataḥ kiṃ tu
sāpavādam

[P 404]
līlotkhāta mṛṇāla kāṇḍa kavala cchedeṣu saṃpāditāḥ
puṣyat puṣkara vāsitasya payasaḥ gaṇḍūṣa saṃkrāntayaḥ
sekaḥ sīkariṇā kareṇa vihitaḥ kāmam virāme punaḥ
na snehāt annarāla nāla nalinī patrātapatram dhṛtam

 katham avadhīraṇā nīrasam vrajati hanta mūḍhaḥ eva asmi yaḥ
 asmin vanecare vayasya Makarandocitam vyavaharāmi hā priya 
 vayasya

[P 405]
dhik ucchvasita vaiśasam mama yat ittham ekākinaḥ
dhik eva ramaṇīyatām tvadanubhāva bhāvāt ṛte
tvayā saha na yāti yaḥ atra divasaḥ api sa dhvaṃsatām
pramoda mṛgatṛṣṇikām dhik aparatra yā jāyate

[P 406]
 Makarandaḥ
aye unmāda mohāntaritaḥ api mām prati kutaḥ cit
 vyañjanāt
prabuddhaḥ vā asya sahaja sneha saṃskāraḥ tat a saṃnihitam
eva mām manyate
puraḥ sthitvā
eṣaḥ pārśva caraḥ eva te Makarandaḥ manda bhāgyaḥ

 Mādhavaḥ
priya vayasya saṃbhāvaya pariṣvajasva mām priyām tu Mālatīm
prati niḥ āśaḥ pariśrāntaḥ asmi
iti mūrchati 

 Makarandaḥ
sa harṣam
eṣaḥ saṃbhāvayāmi jīviteśvaram
vilokya sa karuṇam
hā kaṣṭam āvirbhūta mat pariṣvaṅgotkaṇṭhaḥ 

[P 407]
 eva niḥ cetanaḥ saṃvṛttaḥ tat kṛtam idānīm āśā vyasanena
sarvathā eva me na asti vayasyaḥ iti yuktaḥ paricchedaḥ hā
vayasya

yat sneha saṃjvaravatā hṛdayena nityam
ābaddha vepathu vinā api nimitta yogāt
tvadvyāpadaḥ gaṇayatā bhayam anvabhāvi
tat sarvam ekapade eva mama praśāntam

[P 408]
 sakhe varam te eva atikrāntāḥ muhūrtāḥ tathā vidham api bhavantam
 yeṣu cetayamānam anubhūtavān asmi idānīm tu

bhāraḥ kāyaḥ jīvitam vajra kīlam
kāṣṭhāḥ śūnyāḥ niḥ phalāni indriyāṇi
kaṣṭaḥ kālaḥ mām prati tvat prayāṇe
śāntālokaḥ sarvataḥ jīva lokaḥ

[P 409]
 vicintya
tat kiṃ nu khalu Mādhavāstamaya sākṣiṇā mayā bhavitavyam
iti jīvāmi bhavatu amuṣmāt giri śikharāt Pāṭalāvatyām
 nipatya
Mādhavasya maraṇāgresaraḥ bhavāmi 
kiṃ cit parikramya sa karuṇam nivṛtya avalokya
kaṣṭaṃ bhoḥ kaṣṭam

tat etat asitotpala dyuti śarīram asmin abhūt
mama ati dṛḍha pīḍanaiḥ api na tṛptiḥ āliṅganaiḥ
[P 410]
yat ullasita vismayāḥ bata nipītavatyaḥ purā
nava praṇaya vibhramākulita Mālatī dṛṣṭayaḥ

 āścaryam etasyām tanau a carame vayasi tāvataḥ guṇa 
 samāhārasya saṃniveśaḥ katham iva abhūt sakhe Mādhava

āpūrṇaḥ ca kalābhiḥ induḥ amalaḥ yātaḥ ca Rāhoḥ mukham
samjātaḥ ca ghanāghanaḥ jaladharaḥ śīrṇaḥ ca vāyoḥ javāt
nivṛttaḥ ca phalegrahiḥ druma varaḥ dagdhaḥ ca dāvāgninā
tvam cūḍāmaṇitām gataḥ ca jagataḥ prāptaḥ ca mṛtyoḥ vaśam

[P 411]
 tat pariṣvaje tāvat evam gatam api vayasyam arthitaḥ ca anena
 saṃprati eva ayam arthaḥ
pariṣvajya 
hā vayasya vimala vidyā nidhe guṇa guro Mālatī 
 svayaṃgrāha jīviteśvara Kāmandakī Makarandānandana
 mukha candra Mādhava ayam atra te janmani apaścimaḥ paścimāvasthā 
 prārthitaḥ Makaranda bāhu pariṣvaṅgaḥ sakhe
saṃprati muhūrtam api Makarandaḥ jīvati iti mā evam maṃsthāḥ

[P 412]
ā janmanaḥ sahanivāsitayā mayā eva
mātuḥ payodhara payaḥ api samam nipīya
tvam puṇḍarīka mukha bandhutayā nirastam
ekaḥ nivāpa salilam pibasi iti a yuktam

 sa karuṇam vimucya parikramya ca
iyam adhastāt Pāṭalāvatī bhagavati āpage

priyasya suhṛdaḥ yatra 
mama tatra eva saṃbhavaḥ

bhūyāsam anusaṃcaraḥ

 iti patitum icchati

 Mādhavaḥ
āśvasya

 vicintya

 Mādhavaḥ bhagavan paurastya pavana

[P 415]

virahiṇi jane mūrchām labdhvā vinodayati vyathām
a karuṇa punaḥ saṃjñā vyādhim vidhāya kim īhase

[P 416]
 Makarandaḥ
su vṛttam akhila jantu jīvanena mātariśvanā

 Mādhavaḥ
deva vāyo tathāpi bhavantam evam prārthaye

saha jīvitam vaha mama priyā yataḥ
athavā tadaṅga parivāsa śītalam
mayi kiṃ cit arpaya bhavān hi me gatiḥ

 kṛtāñjaliḥ praṇamati

[P 417]
 Saudāminī
su samāhitaḥ khalu abhijñānārpaṇasya avasaraḥ
 añjalau mālām muñcati

 Mādhavaḥ
sākūta vismaya harṣam
katham iyam asmadviracitā priyatamā stanonnāha 
 duḥ lalita mūrtiḥ Anaṅga mandirāṅgana bakula prasava 
 mālā
 nirūpya sa harṣam
kaḥ saṃdehaḥ tathā hi saḥ eva ayam asyāḥ

[P 418]
mugdhendu sundara tadīya mukhāvaloka 
 helā viśṛṇkhala kutūhala nihnavāya
duḥ nyasta puṣpa racitaḥ api Lavaṅgikāyāḥ
toṣam tatāna viṣama grathitaḥ api bhāgaḥ

[P 419]
 sa harṣonmādam utthāya
priye Mālati iyam vīkṣyase 
sa kopam iva
 ayi madavasthānabhijñe

prayānti iva prāṇāḥ su tanu hṛdayam dhvaṃsate iva
jvalanti iva aṅgāni prasarati samantāt iva tamaḥ
tvarā prastāvaḥ ayam na khalu parihāsasya viṣayaḥ
tat akṣṇoḥ ānandam vitara mayi mā bhūḥ a karuṇā

[P 420]
 sarvataḥ dṛṣṭvā sa nirvedam
kutaḥ atra Mālatī 
bakula mālām uddiśya ayi priyā praṇayini
bakula māle upakāriṇī asi ataḥ svāgatam
bhavatyāḥ

niḥ pratyūhāḥ priya sakhi yadā duḥ sahāḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ
dehoddāha vyasana guravaḥ manmathonmātha vegāḥ
tasmin kāle kuvalaya dṛśaḥ tvat samāśleṣaḥ eva
prāṇa trāṇam praguṇam abhavat mat pariṣvaṅga kalpaḥ

 sa karuṇam nirvarṇya

[P 421]
ānanda miśra madana jvara dīpanāni
gāḍhānurāga rasavanti tadā tadā ca
snehāṅkanāni mama mugdha dṛśaḥ ca kaṇṭhe
kaṣṭam smarāmi tava tāni gatāgatāni

 iti hṛdaye nidhāya mūrchati

 Makarandaḥ
upasṛtya jīvayan 
sakhe samāśvasihi smāśvasihi

āśvasya

[P 422]
 sneham vahataḥ bakula dāmnaḥ lābhaḥ tat katham nu manyase

 upanetrī

samucchvasya

 Makarandaḥ
kaṣṭaṃ bhoḥ kaṣṭam

[P 424]
kumudākareṇa śaradindu candrikā
yadi rāmaṇīyaka guṇāya saṃgatā
su kṛtam tat astu katamaḥ tu ayam vidhiḥ
yat a kāla megha vitatiḥ vyayūyujat

 Mādhavaḥ
hā priye Mālati kaṣṭam ati bībhatsam āpannā asi

 grastā
utpāta dhūma lekhākrāntā iva kalā śaśadharasya

[P 425]
 bhagavati Kapālakuṇḍale

nirmāṇam eva hi tat ādara lālanīyam

naisargikī surabhiṇaḥ kusumasya siddhā
mūrdhni sthitiḥ na musalaiḥ avatāḍanāni

[P 426]
 Saudāminī

ati niṣkaruṇā eva sā

 ubhau
praṇamya
ati prasannam āryā pādaiḥ tat kathaya kā punaḥ tvam asmākam
evaṃvidhā bandhuḥ

utthāya

[P 427]
guru caryā tapaḥ tantra 
 mantra yogābhiyoga jām
imām ākṣepiṇīm siddhim 
ātanomi śivāya vaḥ

 iti sa Mādhavā niṣkrāntā

 Makarandaḥ
āścaryam āścaryam

vyatikaraḥ iva bhīmaḥ tāmasaḥ vaidyutaḥ ca
kṣaṇam upahata cakṣuḥ vṛttiḥ udbhūya śāntaḥ

 vilokya sa bhayam

vicintya 
kim anyat
prabhavati hi mahimnā svena yogin īśvarī iyam
[P 428]

 sa vitarkam kim ayam arthaḥ annarthaḥ iti saṃprati
pramugdhaḥ asmi api ca

a stoka vismayam apasmṛta pūrva vṛttam
udbhūta nūtana bhaya jvara jarjaram naḥ
eka kṣaṇa truṭita saṃghaṭita pramoham
 ānanda śoka śabalatvam upaiti cetaḥ

[P 429]
 tat atra kāntāra gahane saha asmadvargeṇa praviṣṭām
bhagavatīm anviṣya tasyāḥ vṛttāntam enam kathayāmi iti
niṣkrāntaḥ
Mālatyanveṣaṇam nāma navamaḥ aṅkaḥ

[P 430]
 daśamaḥ aṅkaḥ
tataḥ praviśati Kāmandakī Lavaṅgikā Madayantikā ca 

 Kāmandakī
sa karuṇam sāsram
hā vatse Mālati madaṅkālaṃkāriṇi kva asi dehi me
prativacanam 

ā janmanaḥ pratimuhūrta viśeṣa ramyāṇi
āceṣṭitāni tava saṃprati tāni tāni
cāṭūni cāru madhurāṇi ca saṃsmṛtāni
deham dahanti hṛdayam ca vidārayanti

[P 431]
 api ca putri

a niyata rudita smitam virājat
katipaya komala danta kuḍmalāgram
vadana kamalakam śiśoḥ smarāmi
skhalada samañjasa mugdha jalpitam te

 itare
sāsram
 hā[hā] pia [priya ] sahi[ sakhi]
 su pasaṇṇa [su prasanna ] muhānda [mukha candra ]

 pariṇāmo[ pariṇāmaḥ] ekkaliāe[ekākinyāḥ] uvaṇado[upanataḥ]

hā vatsau

ujjihāna jīvitām varākīm na anukampase iyam hi 

ujjvalālokayā snigdhā 
tvayā tyaktā na rājate
malīmasa mukhī vartiḥ 
pradīpa śikhayā yathā

[P 435]
 katham ca kalyāṇi Kāmandakīm parityajasi nanu
a karuṇe madīya 
 cīvaroṣmaṇā eva te praguṇīkṛtāni aṇgāni 

stanya tyāgāt prabhṛti su mukhī danta pāñcālikā iva
krīḍā yogam tat anu vinayam prāpitā vardhitā ca
loka śreṣṭhe guṇavati vare sthāpitā tvam mayā eva
snehaḥ mātuḥ mayi samadhikaḥ tena yuktaḥ tava api

[P 436]
 sa vaiklavyam
candra mukhi saṃprati niḥ āśā asmi

a kāraṇa smera manoharānanaḥ
śikhā lalāṭārpita gaura sarṣapaḥ
tava aṅka śāyī parivṛtta bhāgyayā
mayā na dṛṣṭaḥ tanayaḥ stanaṃdhayaḥ

kim anyat

[P 461]
kāle saṃtata varṣiṇaḥ jalamucaḥ santu kṣitau puṇyataḥ
modantām ghana bandhu bāndhava suhṛdgoṣṭhī pramodāḥ
prajāḥ

[P 462]
 iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve Mālatī mādhave daśamaḥ aṅkaḥ
 iti Mālatī mādhavam nāma prakaraṇam samāptam
